c-6,0,-10,-1,-12,-3s-194,-422,-194,-422s-65,47,-65,47z
M834 80h400000v40h-400000z"></path>
                    </svg></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.1133em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，可以平衡预处理和单次询问的复杂度。</p>
<p>程序中的数组 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>F</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">F[k][n]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.13889em;">F</span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">]</span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span> 保存较小 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span> 对应的 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f(n,k)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。该数组在所有测试开始前统一预处理，因此不需要在多测之间清空。</p>
<h3>处理禁选位置</h3>
<p>先计算不限制位置 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 时的方案数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f(n,k)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>考虑所有选择了位置 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的方案。此时，与 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 距离小于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span> 的位置均不能被选择。</p>
<p>左侧仍可选择的位置为：</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mn>2</mn>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mo>…</mo>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1,2,\ldots,x-k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8389em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="minner">…</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span>，</p>
<p>其长度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x-k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>右侧仍可选择的位置为：</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mo>…</mo>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x+k,x+k+1,\ldots,n</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8389em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="minner">…</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span></span></span></span>，</p>
<p>其长度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-x-k+1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>左右两部分之间的任意两个位置距离均不小于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span>，因此可以独立选择。选择了位置 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的方案数为：</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f(x-k,k)f(n-x-k+1,k)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>最终答案为：</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f(n,k)-f(x-k,k)f(n-x-k+1,k)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>所有运算均对 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>998244353</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">998244353</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">998244353</span></span></span></span> 取模。</p>
<h3>正确性证明</h3>
<p>对于任意不限制位置的合法方案，按照最后一个位置是否被选择，可以唯一划分为两类。</p>
<p>不选择最后一个位置时，方案与前 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 个位置的合法方案一一对应；选择最后一个位置时，之前的 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 个位置不能选择，方案与前 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span> 个位置的合法方案一一对应。因此递推式能够正确计算 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f(n,k)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>组合数公式通过消去相邻被选位置之间必须预留的空位，将原问题一一对应到普通的选点问题，因此也能正确计算 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f(n,k)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>对于一次询问，全部合法方案可以划分为“不选择 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>”和“选择 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>”两类。选择 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 后，剩余可选位置被分成互不影响的左右两段，其方案数为两段方案数之积。用全部方案数减去选择 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的方案数，得到的恰好是不选择 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的方案数。</p>
<p>因此，算法能够正确回答每次询问。</p>
<h3>复杂度分析</h3>
<p>设 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>N</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">N</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10903em;">N</span></span></span></span> 为所有测试中 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n</annotation>
        </semantics>

        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>Q</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">Q</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8778em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">Q</span></span></span></span> 为所有测试中的询问总数。</p>
<p>较小 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">

<p>空间复杂度为：</p>

                    </svg></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.1133em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<h2>1004</h2>
<h3>核心思路</h3>
<p>将两棵树分别称为实际树和地图树。</p>
<p>考虑一个能够将所有实际边染黑的方案。每次成功操作恰好染黑一条新的实际边，因此总共需要 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 次成功操作。</p>
<p>一条地图边在第一次成功使用后，其对应路径中已经存在黑边，之后不能再次成功使用。地图树恰好有 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 条边，所以在完整方案中，每条地图边都恰好成功使用一次。</p>
<p>考虑最后一次成功操作。此时实际树中只剩一条白边，其他边均为黑色。最后选择的地图边对应的实际路径必须全部为白色，因此这条路径只能包含最后一条白边。</p>
<p>所以，最后染黑的边一定同时存在于两棵树中，即它是两棵树的公共边。</p>
<p>由此可以不断选择两棵树的公共边，并在两棵树中同时收缩它：</p>
<ul>
  <li>如果最终收缩成一个点，答案为 <code>YES</code>；</li>
  <li>如果仍有多个点，但已经不存在公共边，答案为 <code>NO</code>。</li>
</ul>
<h3>公共边收缩</h3>
<p>假设 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 是当前两棵树的一条公共边。</p>
<p>地图边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 在实际树中对应的路径只有实际边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，所以使用这条地图边时，只可能染黑实际边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>可以证明，在两棵树中同时收缩 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 不会改变答案。</p>
<p>如果原问题有解，那么地图边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 必然会被成功使用，并染黑实际边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。可以将这次操作移动到整个方案的最后：</p>
<ul>
  <li>原本在它之前执行的操作不受影响；</li>
  <li>原本在它之后执行的成功操作一定不经过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
              <mi>u</mi>
              <mo separator="true">,</mo>
              <mi>v</mi>
              <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>；</li>
  <li>推迟染黑 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
              <mi>u</mi>
              <mo separator="true">,</mo>
              <mi>v</mi>
              <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 只会使更多边保持白色，不会让其他操作失效。</li>
</ul>
<p>删去最后这次操作并收缩 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，即可得到缩点后问题的方案。</p>
<p>反过来，如果缩点后的问题有解，可以先按照缩点后的方案执行操作。将路径还原到原树后，路径可能额外经过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，但这条边始终保持白色，因此不会影响操作成功。最后再选择地图边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，将实际边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 染黑。</p>
<p>所以可以收缩任意一条公共边，而不需要寻找特殊的收缩顺序。</p>
<h3>动态维护两棵树</h3>
<p>收缩过程中，每个当前节点表示若干原始节点组成的连通块。两棵树在同步收缩后仍然都是树。</p>
<p>对于当前两个连通块 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>y</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x,y</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">y</span></span></span></span>，合并两棵树中连接它们的边，并记录一个二进制状态：</p>
<ul>
  <li>状态为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>：只在实际树中存在；</li>
  <li>状态为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mn>2</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>：只在地图树中存在；</li>
  <li>状态为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mn>3</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span>：在两棵树中都存在，是一条公共边。</li>
</ul>
<p>所有状态为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>3</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 的关系都加入队列。队列中可能存在已经删除的旧关系，取出时检查其当前状态即可。</p>
<p>收缩公共边 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>y</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(x,y)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">y</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 时：</p>
<ol>
  <li>删除 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>x</mi>
              <mo separator="true">,</mo>
              <mi>y</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x,y</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">y</span></span></span></span> 之间的公共关系；</li>
  <li>将较小连通块合并到较大连通块；</li>
  <li>遍历较小连通块的所有邻接关系；</li>
  <li>将这些关系的端点修改为较大连通块；</li>
  <li>如果修改后出现两条连接同一对连通块的关系，就将它们合并；</li>
  <li>如果合并后的状态变为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mn>3</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span>，将其加入公共边队列。</li>
</ol>
<h3>邻接关系维护</h3>
<p>每条关系需要同时出现在两个端点的邻接表中。为了支持 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(1)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 删除和修改端点，使用数组实现侵入式双向链表。</p>
<p>对于每条关系，分别记录它在两个端点邻接表中的：</p>
<ul>
  <li>前一条关系；</li>
  <li>后一条关系；</li>
  <li>当前端点。</li>
</ul>
<p>这样可以在 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(1)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 时间内完成以下操作：</p>
<ul>
  <li>将关系从某个端点的邻接表中删除；</li>
  <li>将关系加入另一个端点的邻接表；</li>
  <li>删除整条关系；</li>
  <li>修改关系的一个端点。</li>
</ul>
<p>整个过程中不使用 <code>set</code>、<code>map</code> 或链表节点对象，避免大量动态内存分配。</p>
<h3>手写哈希表</h3>
<p>收缩 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>y</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">y</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">y</span></span></span></span> 到 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 时，对于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>y</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">y</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">y</span></span></span></span> 的每个邻点 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>v</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">v</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span></span></span></span>，需要快速判断当前是否已经存在关系 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(x,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>使用手写拉链哈希表维护无向点对到关系编号的映射。</p>
<p>首先将无向点对编码为一个 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>64</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">64</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">64</span></span></span></span> 位整数：</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>K</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mo>=</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>min</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mo>≪</mo>
            <mn>32</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0.22em" rspace="0.22em">∣</mo>
            <mi>max</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>u</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>v</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">K(u,v)=(\min(u,v)\ll 32)\mathbin{|}\max(u,v)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.07153em;">K</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mop">min</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">≪</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">32</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin"><span class="mord">∣</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mop">max</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">u</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">v</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>然后使用 <code>xorshift</code> 对键进行混合，并对 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>10000019</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">10000019</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">10000019</span></span></span></span> 取模得到桶编号。</p>
<p>哈希冲突使用链表处理，因此冲突不会影响算法正确性，只会影响运行时间。每条关系本身同时作为一个哈希节点，不需要额外分配哈希节点。</p>
<p>哈希表支持：</p>
<ul>
  <li>查询某对连通块之间的关系；</li>
  <li>插入一条新关系；</li>
  <li>删除一条旧关系；</li>
  <li>修改关系端点时先删除旧键，再插入新键。</li>
</ul>
<p>为了避免每组测试都清空全部 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>10000019</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">10000019</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">10000019</span></span></span></span> 个桶，可以使用时间戳记录每个桶属于哪一组测试。首次访问当前测试中的某个桶时，再将桶头初始化为空。</p>
<p>相较于 <code>unordered_map</code>，该实现避免了：</p>
<ul>
  <li>节点级动态内存分配；</li>
  <li>扩容和重新散列；</li>
  <li>装载因子带来的额外空间；</li>
  <li>通用容器较差的缓存局部性。</li>
</ul>
<p>哈希表只影响效率，不影响答案的确定性，这不是随机正确率算法。</p>
<h3>启发式合并</h3>
<p>每次收缩时，将包含原始节点较少的连通块合并到较大的连通块。</p>
<p>假设一个关系端点当前属于大小为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>s</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span> 的连通块，并且这次作为较小的一侧被移动。合并后，它所在的连通块大小至少变为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
            <mi>s</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2s</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>因此，同一个关系端点最多被移动 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>log</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right:0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 次。</p>
<p>初始关系数量不超过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2n-2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>。关系合并后只会减少，不会产生超过初始数量的新关系，所以所有端点移动次数之和为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mi>log</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(n\log n)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right:0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<h3>算法流程</h3>
<ol>
  <li>
    <p>读入实际树，将每条边以状态 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
            <semantics>
              <mrow>
                <mn>1</mn>
              </mrow>
              <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
            </semantics>
          </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 加入关系集合。</p>
  </li>
  <li>
    <p>读入地图树，将每条边以状态 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
            <semantics>
              <mrow>
                <mn>2</mn>
              </mrow>
              <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
            </semantics>
          </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> 加入关系集合。</p>
  </li>
  <li>
    <p>如果某个点对已经存在，则合并状态。</p>
  </li>
  <li>
    <p>将所有状态为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
            <semantics>
              <mrow>
                <mn>3</mn>
              </mrow>
              <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
            </semantics>
          </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 的公共关系加入队列。</p>
  </li>
  <li>
    <p>当剩余连通块数量大于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
            <semantics>
              <mrow>
                <mn>1</mn>
              </mrow>
              <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
            </semantics>
          </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 时：</p>
    <ul>
      <li>从队列中取出一条仍然有效的公共关系；</li>
      <li>如果不存在有效公共关系，输出 <code>NO</code>；</li>
      <li>收缩该公共关系连接的两个连通块；</li>
      <li>更新较小连通块的所有邻接关系。</li>
    </ul>
  </li>
  <li>
    <p>如果最终只剩一个连通块，输出 <code>YES</code>。</p>
  </li>
</ol>
<h3>正确性证明</h3>
<p>下面证明算法输出正确。</p>
<p>首先，在任意完整方案中，每条地图边恰好成功使用一次。因为一条地图边成功使用后，其对应路径中已经存在黑边，不能再次成功使用；而染黑全部 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 条实际边需要 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 次成功操作，地图树也恰好有 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n-1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 条边。</p>
<p>考虑完整方案的最后一次成功操作。此时只有一条实际边仍为白色，最后选择的地图边所对应的实际路径必须全部为白色。因此，该路径只能由最后一条白边组成，说明当前两棵树一定存在公共边。</p>
<p>对于任意公共边，同时在两棵树中收缩它不会改变问题是否可行。原问题的方案可以将这条公共边对应的操作移动到最后，再删除该操作并收缩公共边；缩点后问题的方案也可以还原到原树，最后补上染黑公共边的操作。</p>
<p>因此，算法每次收缩公共边后，当前问题与收缩前问题的答案相同。</p>
<p>如果算法最终将两棵树收缩成一个点，则空问题显然可行。根据每次收缩都保持可行性，原问题也可行，算法输出 <code>YES</code> 正确。</p>
<p>如果算法在仍有多个连通块时找不到公共边，假设当前问题可行，则根据最后一次成功操作的性质，当前两棵树必须存在公共边，与算法维护的结果矛盾。因此当前问题不可行，原问题也不可行，算法输出 <code>NO</code> 正确。</p>
<p>综上，算法始终输出正确答案。</p>
<h3>复杂度分析</h3>
<p>手写哈希表的查询、插入和删除的期望复杂度均为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(1)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>使用按连通块大小启发式合并后，每个关系端点最多移动 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>log</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log n)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right:0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 次。因此，一组测试数据的期望时间复杂度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mi>log</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(n\log n)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right:0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>设哈希桶数量为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>H</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">H</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.08125em;">H</span></span></span></span>，空间复杂度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>H</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(n+H)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.08125em;">H</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。本实现中 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>H</mi>
            <mo>=</mo>
            <mn>10000019</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">H=10000019</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.08125em;">H</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">10000019</span></span></span></span>，在固定数据范围下可视为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>n</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(n)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 额外空间。</p>
<h3>另一种做法</h3>
<p>也可以从第一次成功操作出发，寻找当前只被一条地图路径覆盖的实际边，再使用树上差分、重链剖分和线段树不断删除唯一覆盖关系。</p>
<p>这种做法同样正确，但建模和维护过程更复杂。本题题解与标准实现采用公共边收缩和手写哈希表。</p>
<h2>1005</h2>
<h3>核心思路</h3>
<p>对于长度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span> 的偏好串 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>S</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">S</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.05764em;">S</span></span></span></span>，先将操作过程转化为选择物品集合的问题。</p>
<p>最终可以证明：一个包含 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span> 个物品的集合能否全部售出，只与 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>S</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">S</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.05764em;">S</span></span></span></span> 末尾的极长相同字符段有关。由此可求出单个 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>S</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">S</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.05764em;">S</span></span></span></span> 的最优收入，再使用根号分治统计 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>A</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">A</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">A</span></span></span></span> 的所有子串。</p>
<h3>可行集合</h3>
<p>设最终售出的物品集合为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>X</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.07847em;">X</span></span></span></span>，其中 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi>
            <mi>X</mi>
            <mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi>
            <mo>=</mo>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">|X|=k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.07847em;">X</span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>集合 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>X</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.07847em;">X</span></span></span></span> 可行，当且仅当存在一个未售出的物品 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <msub>
              <mi>p</mi>
              <mrow>
                <mi>k</mi>
                <mo>+</mo>
                <mn>1</mn>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p_{k+1}</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6389em;vertical-align:-0.2083em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3361em;"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mbin mtight">+</span><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2083em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>，并且能将 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>X</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.07847em;">X</span></span></span></span> 排列为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <msub>
              <mi>p</mi>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </msub>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <msub>
              <mi>p</mi>
              <mn>2</mn>
            </msub>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mo>…</mo>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <msub>
              <mi>p</mi>
              <mi>k</mi>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p_1,p_2,\ldots,p_k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3011em;"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.15em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3011em;"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist"
