      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6644em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">3</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.04398em;">z</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> 之和为</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mfrac>
              <mrow>

                  <mi>x</mi>

        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.256em;vertical-align:-0.345em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mopen nulldelimiter"></span><span class="mfrac"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.911em;"><span style="top:-2.655em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span><span style="top:-3.23em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="frac-line" style="border-bottom-width:0.04em;"></span></span><span style="top:-3.394em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">3</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7385em;"><span style="top:-2.931em;margin-right:0.0714em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.5em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size3 size1 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">x</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mbin mtight">−</span><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.345em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose nulldelimiter"></span></span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>设最后一次出现余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>

            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>

      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.0556em;"></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin"><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathrm">mod</span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.0556em;"></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 出现了 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">

        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>c</mi>

          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">c</annotation>
        </semantics>

        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>s</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span> 的左端点都相同。</p>
<p>因此，这部分的总贡献为</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mfrac>
              <mrow>
                <msup>
                  <mn>3</mn>
                  <mi>x</mi>
                </msup>
                <mo>−</mo>
                <mn>1</mn>
              </mrow>
              <mn>2</mn>
            </mfrac>

            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <msup>
              <mn>3</mn>
              <mi>c</mi>
            </msup>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\frac{3^x-1}{2}(1+3^c)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.256em;vertical-align:-0.345em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mopen nulldelimiter"></span><span class="mfrac"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.911em;"><span style="top:-2.655em;"><span class="pstrut" class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="frac-line" style="border-bottom-width:0.04em;"></span></span><span style="top:-3.394em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">3</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7385em;"><span style="top:-2.931em;margin-right:0.0714em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.5em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size3 size1 mtight"><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" <h3>维护信息</h3>
<p>从左到右扫描前缀余数，维护：</p>
<ul>
  <li><code>count[r]</code>：当前余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>r</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">r</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span> 已经出现的次数；</li>
  <li><code>nextCountAtLast[r]</code>：最后一次出现余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>r</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">r</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span> 时，余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
              <mi>r</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
              <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
              <mtext> </mtext>
              <mo lspace="0.22em" rspace="0.22em">
                <mrow>
                  <mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi>
                  <mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi>
                  <mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi>
                </mrow>
              </mo>
              <mtext> </mtext>
              <mn>3</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(r+1)\bmod 3</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.0556em;"></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin"><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathrm">mod</span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.0556em;"></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 已经出现的次数。</li>
</ul>
<p>若当前需要计算最后一个余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>s</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span></span></span></span> 之后，余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>s</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
            <mtext> </mtext>
            <mo lspace="0.22em" rspace="0.22em">
              <mrow>
                <mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi>
                <mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi>
                <mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi>
              </mrow>
            </mo>
            <mtext> </mtext>
            <mn>3</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(s+1)\bmod 3</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.0556em;"></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin"><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathrm">mod</span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.0556em;"></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 的出现次数，则有</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>c</mi>
            <mo>=</mo>
            <mi mathvariant="normal">count</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>s</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi mathvariant="normal">nextCountAtLast</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>s</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">c=\operatorname{count}[s+1]-\operatorname{nextCountAtLast}[s]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">c</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mord mathrm">count</span></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">]</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mord mathrm">nextCountAtLast</span></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>每个右端点只进行常数次运算。</p>
<h3>正确性证明</h3>
<p>任意操作序列都对应原数组的一个连续段划分，每个结果元素等于对应段的元素和模 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>3</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span>；反之，任意连续段划分都可以通过段内合并实现。</p>
<p>对于固定的两个前缀位置，若能产生前导 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>0</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>，则选择该 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>0</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span> 一定优于直接产生 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 或 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>。因此，字典序最小方案会依次经过所有与左端点余数相同的位置。</p>
<p>余数差为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 时，经过所有前导 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>0</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span> 后直接到达终点得到最短的可行序列，因此结果为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <msup>
              <mn>0</mn>
              <mi>z</mi>
            </msup>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0^z,1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8588em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">0</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.04398em;">z</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>余数差为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> 时，如果存在中间余数 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>s</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s+1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>，先产生 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 一定优于直接产生 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>。随后应经过所有余数为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>s</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">s+1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">s</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 的位置，以获得尽可能多的 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>0</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>。如果不存在，则只能直接产生 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>。因此推导出的两种规范形式均为字典序最小数组。</p>
<p>算法枚举每个右端点，并按照左端点与右端点的前缀余数差分别统计所有可能左端点。余数相同的子数组贡献为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>0</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>，余数差为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 或 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> 的子数组贡献分别由两个公式完整统计。</p>
<p>因此，每个子数组的 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi mathvariant="normal">val</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>f</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\operatorname{val}(f)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mord mathrm">val</span></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10764em;">f</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 被统计且仅被统计一次，算法输出正确。</p>
<h3>复杂度分析</h3>
<p>预处理 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>3</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 的幂需要 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>N</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(N)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> 时间。</p>
<p>每组测试数据只需扫描数组一次，时间复杂度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>N</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(N)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.10903em;">N</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，额外空间复杂度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(1)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<h2>1011</h2>
<h3>核心思路</h3>
<p>将黑色集合表示为若干个允许重叠、允许带权的闭区间。</p>
<p>分别维护所有区间左端点和右端点。对于任意坐标 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>，覆盖 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的区间权值和为</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <msub>
              <mo>∑</mo>
              <mrow>
                <mi>l</mi>
                <mo>≤</mo>
                <mi>x</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
            <msub>
              <mi>w</mi>
              <mi>l</mi>
            </msub>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <msub>
              <mo>∑</mo>
              <mrow>
                <mi>r</mi>
                <mo>&#x3C;</mo>
                <mi>x</mi>
              </mrow>
            </msub>
            <msub>
              <mi>w</mi>
              <mi>r</mi>
            </msub>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sum_{l\le x}w_l-\sum_{r&#x3C;x}w_r</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.1449em;vertical-align:-0.3949em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mop op-symbol small-op" style="position:relative;top:0em;">∑</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.1864em;"><span style="top:-2.4003em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mrel mtight">≤</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">x</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3949em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02691em;">w</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3361em;"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:-0.0269em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.15em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0771em;vertical-align:-0.3271em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mop op-symbol small-op" style="position:relative;top:0em;">∑</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.0777em;"><span style="top:-2.4003em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mrel mtight">&#x3C;</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">x</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3271em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02691em;">w</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.1514em;"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:-0.0269em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.15em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>这个值非零，当且仅当 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 为黑色。</p>
<p>使用两棵可持久化随机平衡树分别维护左端点和右端点，即可支持坐标整体平移、区间翻转和历史版本恢复。</p>
<h3>端点表示</h3>
<p>对于一个带权闭区间 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[l,r]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>：</p>
<ul>
  <li>在左端点树中加入坐标 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>l</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span>、权值 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>；</li>
  <li>在右端点树中加入坐标 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>r</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">r</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span>、权值 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>。</li>
</ul>
<p>允许多个区间互相重叠。只要一个整点的总覆盖权值非零，就将其视为黑色。</p>
<p>翻转操作产生的边界修正可能带有负权值，因此节点需要维护一般整数权值，而不只是端点数量。</p>
<h3>单点染黑</h3>
<p>将整点 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 染黑，等价于加入区间 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[x,x]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>因此，分别向左右端点树中插入一个坐标为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>、权值为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 的节点。</p>
<p>如果 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 原本已经是黑色，新的区间会与已有区间重叠，但覆盖权值仍然非零，不会改变颜色。</p>
<h3>扩张黑色区域</h3>
<p>一次半径为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的扩张会使每个黑色区间</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[l,r]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span></p>
<p>变成</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[l-x,r+x]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>因此，只需：</p>
<ul>
  <li>将左端点树中的所有坐标减去 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>x</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>；</li>
  <li>将右端点树中的所有坐标加上 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>x</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>。</li>
</ul>
<p>平衡树维护全树坐标加法懒标记，不需要显式合并扩张后重叠的区间。</p>
<h3>区间翻转</h3>
<p>区间 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[l,r]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span> 内的坐标按照</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>p</mi>
            <mo>↦</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>p</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p\mapsto l+r-p</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7054em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">↦</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span></span></span></span></p>
<p>进行映射。</p>
<p>翻转后：</p>
<ul>
  <li>原来的右端点成为左端点；</li>
  <li>原来的左端点成为右端点；</li>
  <li>区间内端点顺序反转。</li>
</ul>
<p>将两棵树分别按照 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>l</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span> 和 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">r+1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 分裂，取出位于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[l,r]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span> 内的端点。对这部分节点执行顺序翻转、坐标取反，再整体加上 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l+r</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span>，随后交换左右端点树中的对应部分。</p>
<p>若某个黑色区间穿过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>l</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span></span></span></span> 或 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>r</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">r</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span>，其端点不完全位于翻转区间内。此时需要根据区间两侧的覆盖权值，在 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l-1,l,r,r+1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 处加入带权修正端点，使翻转区间与外部颜色正确衔接。</p>
<h3>历史版本</h3>
<p>为第 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>i</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6595em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span> 次操作保存两棵平衡树的根：</p>
<ul>
  <li><code>rtL[i]</code> 表示第 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>i</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6595em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span> 次操作后的左端点树；</li>
  <li><code>rtR[i]</code> 表示第 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>i</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">i</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6595em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">i</span></span></span></span> 次操作后的右端点树。</li>
</ul>
<p>操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>4</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">4</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">4</span></span></span></span> 只需将当前根设为历史版本 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的两个根。</p>
<p>平衡树使用写时复制实现可持久化。修改旧版本中的节点前，先复制该节点，再修改副本，从而保证所有历史版本不受影响。</p>
<h3>单点询问</h3>
<p>询问整点 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 时，分别计算：</p>
<ul>
  <li>左端点树中坐标小于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>x</mi>
              <mo>+</mo>
              <mn>1</mn>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x+1</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> 的权值和，即坐标不超过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>x</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的左端点权值和；</li>
  <li>右端点树中坐标小于 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
          <semantics>
            <mrow>
              <mi>x</mi>
            </mrow>
            <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
          </semantics>
        </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的权值和。</li>
</ul>
<p>两者之差非零时，说明至少有一个带权区间覆盖 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>，输出 <code>Yes</code>；否则输出 <code>No</code>。</p>
<p>询问操作不改变颜色，因此完成临时分裂后，当前版本仍沿用前一个版本的根。</p>
<h3>正确性证明</h3>
<p>左右端点前缀权值之差，等于覆盖当前坐标的所有带权区间权值和。因此，端点结构准确表示黑色集合。</p>
<p>单点染黑加入区间 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mo stretchy="false">[</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>x</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">]</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">[x,x]</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>，准确地使整点 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的覆盖权值增加。</p>
<p>扩张操作将每个区间的左端点减去 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>、右端点增加 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span>，与距离黑色集合不超过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>x</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span></span></span></span> 的定义完全一致。</p>
<p>区间翻转将区间内部所有坐标映射为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo>+</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
            <mo>−</mo>
            <mi>p</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l+r-p</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7778em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span></span></span></span>。交换左右端点、反转顺序并进行坐标映射后，完全位于区间内部的边界均被正确变换；边界修正保证穿过 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>l</mi>
            <mo separator="true">,</mo>
            <mi>r</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">l,r</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.01968em;">l</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">r</span></span></span></span> 的区间在翻转后与区间外颜色正确连接。</p>
<p>可持久化结构保存了每次操作后的完整端点集合，因此操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>4</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">4</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">4</span></span></span></span> 能准确恢复到指定历史状态。</p>
<p>综上，所有操作后维护的颜色状态均正确，询问结果也正确。</p>
<h3>复杂度分析</h3>
<p>随机平衡树的期望高度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>log</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mi>q</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">)</mo>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log q)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right:0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">q</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>1</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>、操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>3</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">3</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">3</span></span></span></span> 和操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>5</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">5</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">5</span></span></span></span> 的期望时间复杂度为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>

            <mi>O</mi>
            <mo stretchy="false">(</mo>
            <mi>log</mi>
            <mo>⁡</mo>
            <mi>q</mi>

          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">O(\log q)</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.02778em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mop">lo<span style="margin-right:0.01389em;">g</span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03588em;">q</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>，操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>

            <mn>2</mn>

          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> 和操作 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">

        <semantics>

<h2>1012</h2>
<p>当 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
            <mo>></mo>
            <mn>2</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k>2</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7335em;vertical-align:-0.0391em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> 时 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mi>k</mi>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">k</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.03148em;">k</span></span></span></span> 是奇数，答案一定为 <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML">
        <semantics>
          <mrow>
            <mn>0</mn>
          </mrow>
          <annotation encoding="application/x-tex">0</annotation>
        </semantics>
      </math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>。</p>
<p>否则统计重边对数即可，使用哈希表可以线性。</p>
